                                        <table border='0' cellpadding='0' cellspacing='0' width='100%' style='margin-top:2px; margin-bottom:7px; border-collapse:collapse;'><tr><td style='padding-top:2px; padding-bottom:6px; border-bottom:1px solid #CBD5E1; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px; text-align:left; line-height:1.4;'><span style='background-color:#1E293B; color:#FF9900; font-size:11.5px; font-weight:800; padding:1px 6px; border-radius:3px;'>&nbsp;01&nbsp;</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;빅테크 컴퓨팅 공급 한계와 2028년 완판 및 초대형 CapEx 회수율 시험대</td></tr></table>앤디 재시 아마존 최고경영자(CEO)가 벤처캐피털 대담을 통해 2026년 컴퓨팅 공급 능력이 이미 전량 판매 완료되었음을 공식 선언함 <a href='https://news-tracker.ywmoon1202.workers.dev/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fn.news.naver.com%2Fmnews%2Farticle%2F023%2F0004005847&amp;title=%EC%95%84%EB%A7%88%EC%A1%B4%20%E2%80%9C%EC%BB%B4%ED%93%A8%ED%8C%85%20%EA%B3%B5%EA%B8%89%2C%20%EC%88%98%EC%9A%94%20%EB%AA%BB%20%EB%94%B0%EB%9D%BC%EA%B0%80%E2%80%A6%202027%EB%85%84%EB%8F%84%20%EB%8C%80%EB%B6%80%EB%B6%84%20%ED%8C%90%EB%A7%A4%E2%80%9D&amp;cat=Summary_Ref' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[1]</a>. 이어 2027년 공급 물량 역시 대부분 판매 완료되었으며 <a href='https://news-tracker.ywmoon1202.workers.dev/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fnews.google.com%2Frss%2Farticles%2FCBMiuwFBVV95cUxOeDBNbTdvV2F5U2tSX1NQWmZ2bnRGUXJMRE4xT1V3V0dIcnlFd3g0WkZ6aWdYNmNzNWxIQXNOcWxMdFlqbFNmN3lWekFUOGJVQzN5aElzeVlveVh4RzZ5aC03b3pSLUNQQ21lQ29jNVkwdmF4V2YwYUlkd0dPdkJQT1VwWF9lZUdhZnBrNWppME81Y2Q2ZzFYOWcwWFozajYyME9BQkpSZHNOTTdQX3hTcXF3RkVSS2s3OU1F%3Foc%3D5&amp;title=Amazon%20cloud%20capacity%20sold%20out%20through%202027%2C%20CEO%20Jassy%20says&amp;cat=Summary_Ref' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[2]</a>, 2028년 가용 물량의 상당 부분까지 사전 예약 계약이 체결된 상태라고 밝힘 <a href='https://news-tracker.ywmoon1202.workers.dev/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.chosun.com%2Feconomy%2Ftech_it%2F2026%2F10%2F25%2F4WAYQ6KLOZBTNB7VREYIFEGJYE%2F&amp;title=%EC%9B%94%EA%B0%80%20%E2%80%98AI%20%EA%B3%BC%EC%9E%89%ED%88%AC%EC%9E%90%E2%80%99%20%EC%9A%B0%EB%A0%A4%EC%97%90%EB%8F%84%E2%80%A6%20%EC%95%84%EB%A7%88%EC%A1%B4%20%E2%80%9C2028%EB%85%84%20%EB%AC%BC%EB%9F%89%EA%B9%8C%EC%A7%80%20%EB%A7%A4%EC%A7%84%E2%80%9D&amp;cat=Summary_Ref' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[3]</a>. 아마존이 올해 계획한 설비투자 규모는 2,200억 달러(약 291.9조 원)에 달하며 <a href='https://news-tracker.ywmoon1202.workers.dev/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fnews.google.com%2Frss%2Farticles%2FCBMidkFVX3lxTFAyNnkzR0QyMFQ4UlZnVThjczJRdHJhRUNCbmIzQ1U1bkxWUjZrV1JYNjg3dllrZWFKZV9nNl9lelZjRDVnNlRycmV2QnVTN2tPVHp0Mzl4eWdLamljZzJMYXZaZTljQzFMMGNQSnF0OFB2MUhCR3c%3Foc%3D5&amp;title=AWS%20Capacity%20Sold%20Out%20Through%202027%20as%20Amazon%20CEO%20Says%20%24220%20Billion%20Buildout%20Stil&amp;cat=Summary_Ref' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[4]</a>, 폭증하는 AI 수요를 맞추기 위해 투자를 지속 확대하겠다는 입장을 표명함 <a href='https://news-tracker.ywmoon1202.workers.dev/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fnews.google.com%2Frss%2Farticles%2FCBMigAFBVV95cUxQMmVJMmFvS0lXMENhVDRiWGZHNV91SDB0OHVwcVFPd2NqYzFDNmxfT005QWRHZWx6NjdVM3ZrU2VZVHpMdUd1N0MzYzAyVlZrVnBodDFRb2VlaHhMVGh2dnFvMDJkUGhEdGhZRUhjZlRxTjJJUm96OVEydEVZMGYwag%3Foc%3D5&amp;title=%EC%95%84%EB%A7%88%EC%A1%B4%20CEO%20%222027%EB%85%84%EA%B9%8C%EC%A7%80%20%ED%81%B4%EB%9D%BC%EC%9A%B0%EB%93%9C%20%EA%B3%B5%EA%B8%89%EB%9F%89%20%EB%8C%80%EB%B6%80%EB%B6%84%20%EB%A7%A4%EC%A7%84%22&amp;cat=Summary_Ref' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[5]</a>. 뱅크오브아메리카(BofA) 또한 전 세계 데이터센터 투자 규모가 올해 1조 달러(약 1,326.9조 원)에서 2028년 2조 1,000억 달러(약 2,786.5조 원)로 두 배 이상 폭증할 것으로 전망함 <a href='https://news-tracker.ywmoon1202.workers.dev/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fnews.google.com%2Frss%2Farticles%2FCBMigwFBVV95cUxNcXFNYW10Rmp1dmlja25oRkxTc2FuY1F4UzR4Z3NUVXgxa1EzQnduUWhYSnA4VHNUN3YwMUU5cHlWdVpmN2hiVmtNQ3JEMWRSZjFlcFA4NmdPUjBFQTF4b1RZdkJWNDNPdUx1MXBDckt3Y0hHVThPNVY0blpZek1qX0p4TQ%3Foc%3D5&amp;title=%EC%95%84%EB%A7%88%EC%A1%B4%20%22%EC%BB%B4%ED%93%A8%ED%8C%85%20%EA%B3%B5%EA%B8%89%2C%20%EC%88%98%EC%9A%94%20%EB%AA%BB%20%EB%94%B0%EB%9D%BC%EA%B0%80%E2%80%A6%202027%EB%85%84%EB%8F%84%20%EB%8C%80%EB%B6%80%EB%B6%84%20%ED%8C%90%EB%A7%A4%22&amp;cat=Summary_Ref' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[6]</a>. 그러나 전력망 연계 지연으로 인해 고가 가속기를 확보하고도 전원을 켜지 못하는 '다크 GPU(Dark GPUs)' 유휴 보관 병목이 심각한 제약 요인으로 부각됨 <a href='https://news-tracker.ywmoon1202.workers.dev/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fnews.google.com%2Frss%2Farticles%2FCBMikgFBVV95cUxPMWdPdnVYLXptTjBCT292QzZWWmkyVUhVNXhnUWJzRnUyUkpLWWVyWVNWQUVJMmdRa01TSUZieFBtR3JhMDYzZkZvclA3U196LTllYXcxMkJrcjBFbWVfQ1REQ2RGS3d5LW1ob0dHRmIzeTkxTjVBTkZncGtocEJmNExlMEQ3M2hqdXB3RkJlbVJtZw%3Foc%3D5&amp;title=%22Dark%20GPUs%22%20at%20Amazon%20show%20the%20limits%20of%20the%20AI%20buildout%3A%20chips%20are%20easy%20to%20buy%2C&amp;cat=Summary_Ref' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[7]</a>. 아마존이 지난 분기 AWS 영업이익을 초과하는 설비투자를 집행함에 따라 잉여현금흐름(FCF) 적자 전환에 대한 시장의 우려가 고조됨 <a href='https://news-tracker.ywmoon1202.workers.dev/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fnews.google.com%2Frss%2Farticles%2FCBMi1gFBVV95cUxQT25WcldmMDYtVDRQTm44dlJZY3FLVHVMa2lOaDJhUzhXaUhTVVhWa3d2NXd4dHFQeVJXXzlYZEJWUWtiRHY1NkdfSjB2NEplRlhuU2FrOTE1dG5YWHhhLXVLZGk2UXg0RjZjeHY0VTZpNW82OWQ1UDkyR19jSVVDTkJkVzNtcjJiLVJad29kM1VtZTB1WHZMM3JHUmNDcEZkT3NodF85M1FWMUZrZ1Nna1NpRVFDQy1fc3ZfMXBkUTBKaFExdHJLdkJqYzU5VHFJeWJlejZn%3Foc%3D5&amp;title=Amazon%20Spent%20More%20on%20Data%20Centers%20Last%20Quarter%20Than%20AWS%20Earned%20in%20Revenue.%20Shoul&amp;cat=Summary_Ref' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[8]</a>. 메타 역시 연간 인프라 설비투자 지출이 1,300억 달러(약 172.5조 원)를 넘어선 가운데 <a href='https://news-tracker.ywmoon1202.workers.dev/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fnews.google.com%2Frss%2Farticles%2FCBMiigFBVV95cUxQTzNKSFZfYTZpSkRFTnMydW5URXdDWksxdWRobHFaWFk5aFVHVnRCZnp2c1RjN0VEMzNTOHY1ZDRGam80TnJTSVppUjJ6ZkZlamxKYlNVR3NUbU56d1N4OVBEWWxickFMWFN1QnluM1hWYTVSY1RoN25sWXN1NzAzeUhlSnNGcV9sQnc%3Foc%3D5&amp;title=Big%20Tech%20Capex%202026%3A%20Meta%20%24130B%2B%20vs%20Tesla%20%2420B&amp;cat=Summary_Ref' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[9]</a> 설비 약정 부채가 1,250억 달러(약 165.9조 원) 급증하며 신용 위험이 부각됨 <a href='https://news-tracker.ywmoon1202.workers.dev/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fnews.google.com%2Frss%2Farticles%2FCBMia0FVX3lxTFA1UXFsS0NjY3FuUk4xS0FvdlhNRG9waHRTRlBvUDRwQXpWZ0llWGFWWXU1QzZKbXIzaFVSb2FYaFQ0OTJ3QkRGd0tIOVFfandQc3QzQk42eUtOZjlwb3plZVd1OV82T3dJR0xv%3Foc%3D5&amp;title=%EB%A9%94%ED%83%80%20%EC%95%BD%EC%A0%95%207%EC%9B%94%EB%B3%B4%EB%8B%A4%201%2C250%EC%96%B5%EB%8B%AC%EB%9F%AC%20%EB%8A%98%EC%96%B4%E2%80%A6AI%20%ED%88%AC%EC%9E%90%20%27%EC%96%BC%EB%A7%88%EB%82%98%20%EC%98%A4%EB%9E%98%2C%20%EB%AC%B4%EC%8A%A8%20%EB%8F%88%EC%9C%BC%EB%A1%9C%27%EC%97%90%20%EC%8F%A0%EB%A6%B0%20%EB%88%88&amp;cat=Summary_Ref' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[10]</a>. 이에 따라 이번 주 예정된 알파벳, 마이크로소프트, 아마존의 분기 실적 발표에서 천문학적 AI CapEx의 실질 현금 회수율 검증이 핵심 승부처가 될 것으로 보임 <a href='https://news-tracker.ywmoon1202.workers.dev/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fnews.google.com%2Frss%2Farticles%2FCBMiVkFVX3lxTFB1RlNxRU15RG9CeFVWQThXbWxBazdYdXZ2N0tIejdtMExrVUNWMHVrc2pEQWpPVDluSURNSnZManRXcm02VzJEck05WnRIbmdVMGNOd0hn0gFbQVVfeXFMT1M4T3lDYnZvNnJFb3Jfcnk0b3ZtTHZZWi0xdi1kTkhqdU84NHRSV1NLNzYya3BUUUpXRTRycjFaejJJeVZMWGpkQkFuOVpaeUU5MGpaZDctcnZZZw%3Foc%3D5&amp;title=%EC%95%8C%ED%8C%8C%EB%B2%B3%C2%B7%EB%A7%88%EC%9D%B4%ED%81%AC%EB%A1%9C%EC%86%8C%ED%94%84%ED%8A%B8%2028%EC%9D%BC%20%EC%8B%A4%EC%A0%81%E2%80%A6%EC%95%84%EB%A7%88%EC%A1%B4%EC%9D%80%2029%EC%9D%BC%20%EB%B0%9C%ED%91%9C&amp;cat=Summary_Ref' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7;
